My wife mentioned it. I just... it feels like I'm moving through mud. Every little thing takes so much effort. It just takes a long time to get them out.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling restless or fidgety lately?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's... like I just said. I'm just slow. My wife mentioned it. I just... it feels like I'm moving through mud. Every little thing takes so much effort. And... when someone asks me something, I hear them, but... it's like I can't find the words. It just takes a long time to get them out.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling restless or fidgety lately?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's... like I just said. I'm just slow. I don't have the energy to be restless. It's just the mud. Everything is just... heavy.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last four months where you felt significantly better for a few days or more?\n\nPatient: No. I haven't. It's just been... the same. Every day.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any days in the past four months where you felt almost back to normal?\n\nPatient: I... no. Like I just said. It doesn't let up. There's no normal anymore.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in or look forward to?\n\nPatient: No. I used to... do things. Choir. Pickup ball. Working on cars, grilling for the family... stuff like that. But... no. I just can't make myself care enough to show up anymore. I don't even answer their texts. There's just... nothing.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange thoughts or heard voices that others couldn't hear?\n\nPatient: I... guess so. I don't really like talking about it. But... yeah. That my family would just be better off without me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
